## Source correspondence

The [exact local QOA source gate](source-screens/qoa-local-square-fragment-parent-gate-v1.json) and [parent source disposition](source-screens/qoa-ordinary-square-source-correspondence-parent-v1.md) bind the prior square/generalized-array correspondence at fixed physical addresses: an ordinary four-family gives a six-party 2-uniform aggregate, but the reverse requires conditional product factorization over the same physical output parties. The [coverage correction](existing-target-disjoint-review-feasibility-results-v1-qoa-coverage-addendum-1.json) prevents that square disposition from being mistaken for coverage of the entire earlier v3 review scope. Under the owner's frontier-first direction the P3/Appendix H cube scope was then audited: the [cube source-audit disposition](dispositions/physics-4-qoa-generalized-cube-source-audit-parent-v1.md) adopts, at exact scope, the existence equivalence with corrected trace subscripts, the falsity of the literal row-level one-to-one wording (parent d=5 and reviewer d=8 exact witnesses), the phase dependence of Definition 4.1, and the exact d=8 all-cells-entangled generalized four-square showing that generalized existence does not yield an ordinary product-valued four-MOQLS(8). The earlier metadata-only [range locator](source-screens/qoa-appendix-h-structural-range-v2-receipt-v1.json) and the stopped [reviewer screen](source-screens/qoa-p3-fresh-reviewer-screen-outcome-v1.json) with its [clarification](source-screens/qoa-p3-fresh-reviewer-uncertainty-clarification-v1.json) remain historical records. Whole-paper status and novelty remain NR; no ordinary nonclassical four-MOQLS(8) witness or no-go follows.

The [exact local PMLR gate](source-screens/privacy-pmlr-local-parent-comparison-gate-v1.json), [21-page visual receipt](source-screens/privacy-pmlr-main-visual-receipt-v1.json) and [publisher-model disposition](source-screens/privacy-pmlr-theorem-to-model-parent-v1.md) distinguish the compatible full-shift additive privacy model from the incompatible FIRST-norm objective. Same-l1-radius second moments survive angular averaging, but complete squared-l1 attainment and the registered diamond/squared-Euclidean optimum/equality/selector are not established by this source. NR-K17, NR-K10 and discretization/limit proof cuts remain; no worldwide novelty or general primary restart is inferred.

The [exact local preview-book gate](source-screens/preview-book-literal-local-review-gate-v1.json) and [parent visual observation](source-screens/preview-book-literal-parent-visual-observation-v1.json) compare the supplied transcription with the three retained printed pages. The path-norm expectation ratio, strict sign, displayed controller arguments and both horizon descriptions are printed; detailed process-space and margin conventions remain explicit interpretations. This is a parent-only page-limited comparison, not independent review, a source-wide absence finding or a synthesis theorem. Two earlier reviewers failed exposure screening; [three further status-only screens](source-screens/preview-book-literal-reviewer-screen-round2-outcome-v1.json) returned UNCERTAIN for held-preview exposure, so no reviewer was assigned and no new source read followed.

The [bounded physics-volume contents locator](source-screens/p44-physics-volume-contents-locator-receipt-v1.json) did not establish an exact P44 source page; this is not evidence that the question is absent from the volume. The proposed [SPR statement audit](info-2-wang-yu-statement-audit-prospective-source-screen-stop-v1.json) was stopped under the then-current admission rules; the [owner-reported source-identity frontier](info-2-wang-yu-source-screen-recipient-frontier-addendum-1.json) does not clear its held method. Fresh prospective public-source reads are governed by the later [owner direction](contract-addendum-2-frontier-first-owner-direction-v1.json).

The owner-requested parallel book-literal program and its subsequent registration assessments are **HELD/ZERO/NONINPUT** under the [parent source-boundary stop](parent-parallel-book-program-source-order-containment-v1.json), [B4 separation stop](parent-b4-known-recipient-exposure-process-major-v1.json) and [rerender-order stop](parent-book-rerender-freeze-order-stop-v1.json). Earlier statements in this section treating its five registration-delta dispositions as adopted, or asserting what the printed problems do or do not follow from prior results, are withdrawn; no new claim about their relation is made here. The existing scientific ledger retains its prior exact scopes and qualifications. Frozen historical records and payloads are preserved, not re-read or relabelled.

## Supervision and continuity

Parent must actively process returns and allocate specific admitted next steps, with real native-clock substantive checkpoints<=30min apart; gaps remain disclosed. Do not count assignments, parked tasks, heartbeat loops or a live service as continuous research. Independent-model CLI review: **process completed; return HELD/ZERO/NONINPUT, not a usable foundation review**. The owner [authorized](independent-cli/authorization-and-public-admission-v1-addendum-1.json) transmission of exactly six unpublished prior-round semigroup manuscripts; the [frozen manifest](independent-cli/review-manifest-v1.json) pins the prompt, schema and requested model. The prompt omitted required before-source overlays: the [source-order stop](independent-cli/review-1-overlay-source-order-major-stop-v1.json) quarantines the entire return and its consumers, superseding the earlier MINOR label and automatic-composition language. The separate [out-of-gate metadata stop](parent-public-source-cli-review-metadata-exposure-stop-v1.json) holds its public-source selection episode and qualifies the 84-path census. Under a separately frozen [metadata-only capture gate](independent-cli/review-1-metadata-only-outcome-gate-v1.json), the [actual launch receipt](independent-cli/launch-receipt-v1.json) and [verified process outcome](independent-cli/review-1-process-outcome-v1.json) establish child exit 0, observed model `claude-opus-5-5`, provider result `success` and matching hashes. The mathematical payload was not interpreted, no finding was admitted, no author was contacted, and no hold was released. No rerun or automatic repair is authorized.

The [pending-review boundary census](review-queue-boundary-census-20261027-v1.json) records ten returned reviews whose parent integrations have named admission barriers, plus the separately held w45 assessment. This is an as-of snapshot, not a claim that the author mathematics is false or that every route is exhausted. The [rolling slot census](report-slot-ledger-census-20261027-v1.json) counts ledger rows only; those counts are not problem completions or scientific-progress scores.
